sim.ts: (the beast) Let me write it now mentally completely.

ts


import { Rng, makeRng, next, range, chance, pick, intRange } from './rng'
import * as B from './balance'
export const STEP = 1 / 60
export interface SimConfig { seed: number; spawnRadius: number }
export interface InputState { mx: number; my: number }
export const K_WISP = 0 ... K_BOSS = 6 (enum-ish consts)
export interface Enemy { id, kind, x, y, vx, vy, hp, maxHp, r, spd, dmg, xp, elite, t1, t2, ph, flash, orbCd, kx, ky, dirX, dirY, spawnT, q }
// q = query stamp for dedupe
export interface Proj { x,y,vx,vy,dmg,pierce,r,life,wid:string,crit:boolean, seedA }
export interface EBullet { x,y,vx,vy,dmg,r,life }
export interface Gem { x,y,val,vx,vy,t,tier } // tier 0 xp-small,1 xp-big,2 heal
export interface Turret { x,y,ttl,cd,imgA? }
export interface WeaponInst { id: string; lvl: number; cd: number; aux: number; aux2: number }
export type EventType = ... union with payload { t: string ... } — simpler: interface Ev { t:number; x:number;y:number; a:number; b:number; s:string } where t is enum number — avoids union allocations? Events rare; use simple object with type tag + optional fields as a single interface with defaults. Discriminated union is cleaner for consumers. But allocation per event... fine (sparse). I'll do: type Ev = { t: 'shot'|'hit'|'die'|'gem'|'hurt'|'level'|'draft'|'surge'|'bossIn'|'bossDead'|'boom'|'beam'|'tpick'|'dash'|'over'|'win'|'turret'; x?:number; y?:number; x2?:number; y2?:number; pts?: number[]; n?: number; s?: string; f?: boolean }
Player fields... 
SimState {
  rng: Rng; seed; time; step;
  arena: number; spawnRadius;
  phase: 'playing' | 'draft' | 'over' | 'won';
  endless: boolean; bossCount: number; bossKilledAt: number;
  nextBossAt: number; nextSurgeAt: number;
  player: Player;
  enemies: Enemy[]; enemyPool: Enemy[];
  projs: Proj[]; projPool: Proj[];
  ebul: EBullet[]; ebulPool: EBullet[];
  gems: Gem[]; gemPool: Gem[];
  turrets: Turret[]; turretPool: Turret[];
  events: Ev[];
  draft: DraftOption[] | null; pendingLevels: number;
  stats: Stats;
  banner: { text: string; sub: string; t: number; ttl: number } | null;
  // spatial grid
  gridHead: Int32Array; gridNext: Int32Array; gridDim: number; gridCell: number; queryStamp: number;
  paradoxUsed: Set<string>? — allocation ok (setup only) — use Record<string,boolean> plain object.
  slow: directorBudget: number;
}
Enemy cap 420. Grid: cell 96, dim = ceil((arena+200)/cell) both axes. heads Int32Array(dim*dim), next Int32Array(cap+4). Insert enemies each step: head.fill(-1).

Separation pairwise within 3x3 cells with id ordering guard; positional push.

stepSim(s, inp):



if (s.phase !== 'playing') return
s.step++; s.time += STEP
const p = s.player
// timers
if (p.iframes > 0) p.iframes -= STEP
p.hurtPulse decay? presentation.
// regen
if (p.regen > 0) p.hp = Math.min(p.maxHp, p.hp + p.regen * STEP)
// movement
const sp = p.spd
p.x += (inp.mx * sp + p.kx) * STEP ... player knock? enemies knocked, player rarely. add small knock on hit for feel: p.kx.
clamp arena ±(arena/2 - r)
facing update if moving (for lancer): p.face = atan2(my, mx) when len>0.1
// director
spawnDirector(s)
// boss spawn check
if (!s.endless ? time>=360 : time>=nextBossAt) && no active boss → spawnBoss
// surges
if (time >= nextSurgeAt) { surge(s); nextSurgeAt += 45 }
// weapons
for w of p.weapons: tickWeapon(s, w)
// turrets
// projectiles
// ebullets
// enemies AI + separation + contact
// gems
// xp/level: handled on collect (gainXp): if p.xp >= p.xpNeed → carry, pendingLevels++, then if phase playing && !draft → openDraft
if (p.hp <= 0) → phase 'over', event
gainXp(s, v): v = p.xpMul; p.xp += v; while p.xp >= p.xpNeed: p.xp-=p.xpNeed; p.level++; p.xpNeed = B.xpNeed(p.level); pendingLevels++; event 'level'.

openDraft: s.draft = B.buildDraft(s) — buildDraft needs rng + player ownership → implement in sim (uses balance tables). phase 'draft'; event 'draft'.

applyDraftPick(s, i): apply option: weapon → existing? lvl++ : weapons.push({id, lvl:1, cd:0.1, aux: rng angle}); check MAX via draft eligibility already. passive → p.pass[id]++, recompute; paradox → effects + mark used; patch → heal/dmg small. pendingLevels--; s.draft = null; if pendingLevels>0 → build new draft stay in draft; else phase 'playing'. Event 'tpick'.

recompute(p): derived: dmgMul, rateMul, spd, maxHp(keep ratio heal by delta when base grows? add flat +22 and also +22 hp), pickup, regen, armor, crit, xpMul, paradox adjustments. Compose: base × (1+…) etc.

Weapons tick (w.cd -= STEP; if cd <= 0 { cd += interval / p.rateMul (weapon rows use base interval); fire }):

Pulse: target = nearestEnemy(s, p.x, p.y, 650); if none skip fire (cd = 0.05 retry soon). for i<n: aim at ith-nearest? find up to n nearest — to keep cheap: get n nearest via scanning enemies with partial selection (n ≤ 3): loop enemies maintaining top-3 distances arrays (scratch). Then projectile each aimed, slight spread. dmg roll crit.

Volt: primary = nearest in 460; chain: from target, repeatedly find nearest enemy within jump 175 from last, not already visited (mark via e.q = stamp). Damage each with falloff^i. Build pts array for beam event [x0,y0,x1,y1,...]. kills along the way possible → careful: damageEnemy may remove enemy mid-iteration; collect chain positions first while applying damage — death just adds events; iteration uses positions captured; fine but querying grid for "nearest within jump excluding visited": use grid query each hop with stamp-exclusion. Complexity moderate; simpler: chain via scanning all enemies each hop (enemies ≤420, hops ≤8 → 3400 dist checks occasionally — fine, no grid needed).

Nova: n bullets radial from player angle offset rotating (w.aux += golden angle each volley).

Drones: continuous: w.aux += rotSTEP (angle); positions p + dir(a + istep)(R); for each drone, grid query enemies within r_drone+e.r → if e.orbCd <= 0 → damageEnemy + knockback outward + e.orbCd = 0.28 + small event 'hit' (limit event spam: orbit hits push 'hit' event with n=dmg; renderer shows damage number; hundreds/hr fine).

Lancer: fire toward p.face (or facing of movement); proj kind 'lancer' with pierce 999, r 8 (collide uses dist point-segment? just circular radius 10, looks broad). L4+: also opposite dir.

Mortar: choose target: sample up to 6 random enemies (int range over enemies array), pick one with most neighbors within 190 (grid count cheap), fallback nearest. Spawn arcing proj: fields: use Proj with vx,vy toward target at speed = dist/travel(0.55s), plus z scale presentation via proj.seedA storing total life & kind 'mortar' (renderer draws arc using life fraction). On expiry (life<=0) → explode: AoE dmg via grid query r aoe, event 'boom'. Mortar doesn't collide mid-air (pierce 999, no collision checks, kind flag skip collide).

Turret: w.cd drop interval → if turrets (owned count by this weapon? store in w.aux2 count?) Find active turrets for player overall (turrets shared): spawn up to max per weapon level (max field). Turret entity: cd fires bolt at nearest, dmg from weapon row... store dmg/rate on turret at spawn (b,c fields? give Turret {x,y,ttl,cd,dmg,rate,range}). Movement doesn't move turret (stationary). ttl expiry → event small.

Actually simpler: turret weapon row: {dmg(bolt), interval: dropInt, a: maxCount, b: ttl, c: boltRate} and range fixed 400.

Enemy bullets: move, if hit player (dist < r + p.r) && iframes<=0 → hurt(p, dmg) delete bullet; life; out of arena delete.

hurt(s, dmg): apply armor: d = max(1, dmg - p.armor); p.hp -= d; p.iframes = 0.5; p.kx += push from source? keep none; event 'hurt' n=d; stats.dmgTaken += d; shake via event consume. if hp<=0 set phase over (at end of step) — set immediately, event 'over', stats finalize.

damageEnemy(s, e, dmg, wid, kx, ky, critCan): crit roll if critCan: dmg*=p.critMul. e.hp -= dmg; e.flash = 0.09; e.kx += kx; stats.dmgDealt += d; perWeapon[wid]+=d; event 'hit' {x,y,n:frounded, f:crit}; if e.hp<=0 killEnemy(s, e) — removal via swap-remove requires index: pass index when iterating; for grid-query kills (drones affecting multiple), mark dead (hp<=0) and do removal in a sweep after enemies AI step: pattern: a removal pass: iterate enemies backwards; if hp<=0 → onDeathEffects + remove. Cleaner. onDeathEffects(s, e): stats.kills++; event 'die' {x,y,n:kind,f:elite}; drop gem(s) (cap check 600 → convert oldest? just skip if full); cell split spawn wisps; elite drop heal; maybe heal orb chance; boss → boss death handling: phase 'won' or endless counters, event 'bossDead', banner.*

Because kill happens via sweep, ensure code after damaging doesn't use stale entity references (copy needed fields before marking).

Enemy AI tick: by kind:

chase kinds: dir = norm(player - e); e.vx = dir*spd (plus slight wobble by id/time? skip).
raptor: ph0: if dist<340 && t1<=0 → ph=1, t1=0.5, store dirX/Y, event 'dash' (telegraph). ph1: t1-=STEP; vel ~ 0 (slight drift); at t1<=0 → ph=2 t1=0.34; ph2: vel = dir*780; t1...→ ph=3 t1=0.5 (vel *= 0.2); → ph0 with cooldown t2 = 1.2.
spitter: maintain ring: radial err; strafe by sin(time0.9 + id1.7); t1 fire cd.
boss: as designed; store patterns in t1 (fan cd), t2 (charge cycle), ph (substate), aux fields charge dir in dirX/dirY. enrage check each tick. Separation applied to non-boss enemies (boss pushes others out instead — boss part of grid, others avoid; boss doesn't move by separation: flag skip if kind boss, but others get pushed by boss since pair resolves both directions — apply full overlap to other, none to boss: special-case). Contact: after separation, if dist(e, player) < e.r + p.r - 2 && p.iframes <= 0 → hurt(e.dmg), p.iframes set; boss contact same.*
Enemy spawn: position ring: ang = next(rng)2π; r = spawnRadius (config, ~max(760, view diag)); x = p.x + cosr clamp arena. elites by eliteChance. spawnT = 0 (scale-in 0.35s, no damage collision while spawnT<0.1? keep collision but enemies can't hurt for first 0.3s: check spawnT > 0.3 for contact. Also spawn event for fx ring spawn particles (n=kind). events 'spawn'? could be spammy (4/s) — renderer shows subtle ring; ok-ish but each event allocs; 4/s fine. I'll add 'spawn' event with pos+r colour for a brief pulse ring — manageable.*

hp scaling at spawn: hp = base * B.hpScale(time) * (elite?7), also xp val for elites 25 flat, dmg scale * B.dmgScale(t).*

Spawn costs from defs; budget s.dirBudget += rate(t)STEP (budget cap 8 prevent burst debt). pick random kind among unlocked weighted w = 1/cost with brute favored later... weighted by cost^ -0.9 with unlock check; budget -= cost. If none affordable / cap → skip. During boss: rate = 0.5.

Also global alive cap 400: skip spawn if above.

Surge: ring N = 12 + floor(time/40) (cap 30) mix wisp/mite (cell after 90s few), at r = spawnRadius*0.85 around player, banner set {text:'SIGNAL SURGE', sub:'hostiles converging'}, event 'surge'.

Boss handling in endless: after continueEndless: bossCount++, nextBossAt = time + 150; boss spawn function uses hp mult by 1.6^bossCount.

Won: when boss dies: stats; if !endless → phase='won' event 'win'; banner 'SIGNAL RESTORED'; else (endless) → banner 'ARCHIVIST DOWN', award heal, nextBossAt = max(nextBossAt, time+120)... Actually in endless, boss death → just continue; set bossActive false; nextBossAt = time + 150.

bossActive flag: s.boss: { id } | null — find by scanning? Store bossActive boolean and boss index ref may change with swap-remove: keep boss in dedicated field s.bossEnemy: Enemy (not in enemies array)! Simpler: boss as separate entity processed in its own code path, but then grid queries (projectile collision) must also test boss — add explicit boss check in each collision query (few places: proj loop, drones, nova is proj, mortar AoE, volt chain include boss? yes). Hmm — mixing semantics: I'll keep boss inside enemies[] (kind 6) so all collision code works uniformly; track s.bossOn flag + locate via scan when needed for HUD hp (scan 400 entries / frame fine) or store id and verify... just scan: boss = enemies.find(kind===6) once per frame in renderer (fine).

Draft builder:



pool: {opt, weight}
for each weapon def: inst = find; if inst && inst.lvl<5 → push {weapon, lvl+1, w:1}; else if !inst && p.weapons.length<4 → push {lvl 1, w: 0.95}
passives similarly (count owned < 4 for new) w 0.9
paradoxes: if !used && time > 100 && (weapons+passives owned>=3) → w 0.45
picks: 3 distinct by weighted sampling w/o replacement (rng).
fill remainder with patch cards: 'patch-heal' (Heal 50), 'patch-armor' (+1 armor, reusable), 'patch-dmg' (+6% dmg this run) weights equal.
DraftOption shape: { kind: 'weapon'|'passive'|'paradox'|'patch'; id: string; lvl: number; name: string; tag: string; desc: string; rarity: 0|1|2 } rarity: 0 common(neon cyan), weapon new = 1? Use rarity for styling: weapons lvl1 'NEW'; paradox 'PARADOX' amber.

desc text built via B.descFor(kind,id,lvl).

applyPick implementation straightforward w/ recompute.

Patch applications: heal: p.hp = min(max, +50); armor+1: p.bonusArmor++; dmg+6%: p.bonusDmg = 1.06 — incorporate in recompute as separate factors (p.bonusArmor, p.bonusDmg, p.bonusRate? keep armor & dmg only).

Paradox applications: set flags p.paradox[id]=true in a Record; effects in recompute:

glass: dmg ×1.35, maxHp −15 (min 40): maxHp = base + aegis22 - 15glass...

regen = .75*mesh

Order per frame in App:



const now = performance.now()
dtReal = clamp(now - last, 0, 66)/1000
if (!paused && phase playing-ish) { acc += dtReal; n=0; while acc>=STEP && n<5 { stepSim(sim, inputRef) ; n++; acc-=STEP } if n===5 acc=0 }
renderer.render(sim, inputView, dtReal, paused||phase!=='playing', now)
audio.update(sim, dtReal)  // music scheduler uses ctx.currentTime internally
sim.events.length = 0
if phaseChanged → setReactPhase

But note: phase 'draft' | 'over' | 'won': also skip stepping. stepSim itself guards too.

Guard: typeof window + AudioContext; if unsupported → no-op impl. Master: gain 0.9 → compressor → destination. sfxBus gain 0.8 → master. musicBus gain 0.5 → master. Delay feedback on arp: delayNode 0.28 fb 0.35 wet 0.3 within music chain (create once). update(s):

if phase over → stop scheduling? play downsting once (flag) and let pad continue quietly? Stop scheduler (sounding=false) + play pacify. win → victory arp once; keep pad. scheduling 16th: idx step%16; bar = step/16 %4; chord midi [45,41,36,43]; quality minor/major offsets: arp pool = [r, r+3, r+7, r+10, r+12] pentatonic minor; pattern deterministic table ARP_PAT = [0,-1,2,-1,1,-1,3,4...] pick via (step7+bar)%len? Use fixed pattern array len 16 with indexes into pool + 12 octave lifts. pad: schedule at bar boundaries long tones: at step%16===0: spawn 2 osc detune sustain 16spb+0.1 through lowpass (cutoff by intensity), gain env 0.05 attack 1.2s release. bass: steps 0,4,8,12 (quarters): sine 0.25s root-12 vel .18 + when int>0.55 add kick (sine drop) same steps; hats: steps%2===0 noise hp 6k vol .04+; snare: int>0.72 steps 4,12 noise bp 1800 vol .1. arp gate: int>0.15 && pattern[idx]>=0 && rnd? no rnd — pattern determined; density by intensity thresholds mapping indexes. Careful details:

Enemy.id increments for pair ordering.
Grid: cell 96, gridDim = Math.ceil((ARENA + 400)/96); world coords from -ARENA/2-margin to +. map x → gx = floor((x + ARENA/2 + 200)/96), clamp.
insert(e): idx = gx + gy*dim; next[i] = head[idx]; head[idx] = i where i = enemy array index (indices valid only within the step since array mutates — rebuild grid at the point of use each step and only use while array not mutated... but damage during queries can mark hp<=0 (no removal until sweep) — array not modified during queries (removal deferred to sweep) EXCEPT cell split spawns new enemies (push only, doesn't move others) — push doesn't invalidate indices. OK: removals ONLY in sweep phase.
For projectile collision, projectiles move and query. Enemy indices used from grid remain valid.
Separation: iterate enemies i, check 3x3 cells, j>i by id comparison (use array index j position stored via grid next — compare stored index jIdx > i to apply pair once (array index order fine).
Enemy struct includes nextIdx? gridNext separate array indexed by enemy index. Enemy array length ≤ ENEMY_CAP+margin( boss +1) — gridNext size cap+8.
Sweep: iterate i from end: if hp<=0 do death effects; swap-remove: enemies[i]=enemies[len-1]; pop; pool push the object that was popped... careful: enemies[i] now holds the last element object; the removed object ref = popped. Implementation:


const e = enemies[i]
onDeath(s, e)
const last = enemies.pop()! // if i === len-1, last === e
pool.push(e)             // recycle dead object
if (i < enemies.length) enemies[i] = last
That pushes e back; e reused later. Fine.

Contact damage: player iframes gate all contact; after hurt, break out (one hit per iframe window).
Enemy spawnT += dt in AI loop; contact allowed if spawnT > 0.25.
Mortar target scoring: candidate selection: if enemies empty skip; sample 6 random indices; count neighbors via grid within mortar aoe radius (use countNeighbors(ex, ey, r)) choose best count; fallback nearest.
Volt chain: visited marking with e.q = stamp, stamp = ++s.queryStamp dedicated: I use single queryStamp counter for all dedupe (volt + orbital? orbital doesn't need dedupe... grid queries for orbitals gather nearby with stamp).
Projectile vs enemies: since enemies aren't removed mid-step, no stale index issues.
Enemy bullets hurt player once then die (set life 0 & inactive via swap? ebullets are plain objects pooled with swap-remove during iteration backwards).
Gems: attracted within pickup2 → velocity toward player accelerating; collect if dist < pickup0.32+12? simpler dist < 26. Collected: xp or heal event.
gainXp: handles level ups & sets pendingLevels & stats. At end of stepSim: if s.pendingLevels>0 && s.phase==='playing' && !s.draft → open draft. Phase guarded at top: if s.phase !== 'playing' return.

damageEnemy(s, e, amount, wid, kbx, kby, canCrit):



let d = amount
let crit = false
if (canCrit && next(rng) < p.crit) { d *= p.critMul; crit = true }
d = Math.round? keep float; e.hp -= d
e.flash = 0.09
e.kx += kbx; e.ky += kby (scaled by kind mass: boss/brute resist)
stats.dmgDealt += d; per weapon map add.

Renderer structure:

ts


import * as B from './balance'
import { SimState, findBoss } from './sim'
import { Rng, makeRng, next, range, TAU, clamp, lerp, damp } from './rng'
export type Quality = 'high' | 'med' | 'low'
export interface StickView { on: boolean; sx: number; sy: number; kx: number; ky: number }
export interface Renderer {
  render(s, stick, dtReal, paused, nowMs): void
  renderMenu(nowMs): void
  resize(wCss, hCss): void
  setQuality(q): void
  reset(): void
  setShakeEnabled(b): void
  getViewSize(): {w,h}
}
createRenderer(canvas): Renderer
Internals:

ctx, w, h (css px), dpr
sprites
camera {x, y, trauma}
pools: particles (typed arrays), shocks (objects), texts (objects), beams (objects)
trail buffer
presentation rng (own seeded instance — not the sim one)
hurtFlash, vignette cached bmp, nebula blobs positions, star tiles
Sprite baking: at init (canvas document available). All sprites as HTMLCanvasElement.

Helper: function spr(size, fn): HTMLCanvasElement.

Colors from B.COLORS.

Enemy sprite per kind: size ~ 64 (elite scaled via drawImage scale anyway; bake elite hoop separately at 96? For elites: draw eliteHoop sprite (glow ring violet) at 1.5x plus base sprite). Bake flash variant (white silhouette) per kind.

Shape functions on ctx at center (s = half-size, use unit coords):

wisp: 4-point diamond star: path [(0,-1),(0.22,-0.22),(1,0),(0.22,0.22),(0,1),(-0.22,0.22),(-1,0),(-0.22,-0.22)] scaled.
mite: triangle pointed up; rotation drawn toward movement.
cell: circle r .7 + inner nucleus circle r .25 filled color.
raptor: long arrow: [(1,0),(-0.7,0.55),(-0.35,0),(-0.7,-0.55)].
spitter: pentagon.
brute: hexagon with thicker rim + inner hex.
boss handled custom live (bigger): bake glow disc 256; arcs rotating live; bake boss core sprite.
Bake each: shadowBlur can be used freely at bake time (cheap at runtime): glow = radial gradient circle drawn behind: simplest robust glow: radial gradient stops [(0, color a=.9),(.35,color a=.5),(1, transparent)].

Sprite drawing function for enemy kind:



function bakeEnemy(kind, color): {base, flash}
size = 72 (pad for glow), unit u = size*0.30 (shape half-extent ≈ 0.30*size → shape ~ 22px at scale 1 for r 12? Sprite drawn scaled to (e.r * 2.6) → collision radius covers core. glow extends.
Draw: gradient fill disc; dark fill shape (#0a0e1a); stroke shape 2.5px color; inner dot.
flash: same shape filled white + white glow.
Orbital blade: capsule cyan-green glow. Bolt sprites: horizontal capsule, white core, colored glow, per weapon colors: bolt(0) cyan, nova(1) sky, lancer(2) long white, turret(4) teal, mortar(3) amber circle. eBullet: magenta-red glow orb. gem small green diamond, big amber diamond, heal pink plus-sign. Player: core white-cyan disc sprite 72; plus live rotating ring (stroke arcs). Star tile: 512 tile with small dots (bake with own rng). Vignette: radial dark edges — will prerender at resize (needs w/h): bake vignette canvas w×h (css px) with radial gradient transparent center→ rgba(2,3,8,.55) edges; red vignette similar with red edges for hurt/low hp.

Particles Pool: Float32Arrays capacity 768 (quality high): px,py,vx,vy,life,max,size + col Uint8. spawn(x,y,count,opts{color,speed,life,size,dir?,spread}). update(dt): integrate, drag, life; draw: 'lighter' discs drawn via drawImage of particle sprites[colIdx] scaled size*lifeFrac. Particle sprite set: colors array of 7 strings → baked discs size 32 (white core color glow).

colors order for particles: 0 cyan #6ef7ff, 1 pink-red #ff3d7f, 2 amber #ffd166, 3 green #57ffa1, 4 violet #c44dff, 5 white #eaf6ff, 6 orange #ff9e4a, 7 blue #7aa8ff.

Shocks: {x,y,r0,r1,t,ttl,lw,colIdx} 24 ring buffer. draw stroke arc expanse ease-out alpha (1-t/ttl).

Texts: {x,y,t,ttl,str,size,crit} 80 ring. rise 30px/s, fade.

Beams: {pts: Float32Array up to 210, n, t, ttl} 12. Draw jagged: for segments draw polyline with jitter 6px using pseudo rand from (t60+i) sin; two passes: wide alpha glow + thin white core; fade.

Telegraphs (dash): {x,y,dx,dy,t,ttl,len,w} 12 — draw translucent lane: from events 'dash'.

Camera shake: trauma decay 1.8/s; offset = trauma^2 * 14 * (noise via sin(now73.7)+sin(now41.3)); rotation skip.

render() flow:



compute cam target = player pos; cam lerp damp(6, dt) (if snap → set)
ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0)
fill bg #04050c
camX += shakeX etc; worldToScreen: translate(w/2 - camX, h/2 - camY)
draw nebula blobs (world coords, 'lighter' alpha .5)
draw star tiles parallax: for depth in [0.35, 0.6]: offset = -cam*depth mod 512; tiled drawImage alpha
draw grid lines
draw arena boundary
draw gems
draw turrets
draw enemies (+elite hoop, flash, spawn pop, elite hp bar small)
draw boss extras (telegraph lane when winding, enrage ring, boss bar later in HUD)
draw orbitals
draw projectiles
draw ebullets
draw player (trail first, then glow, core, ring, iframe shimmer flicker)
draw beams, telegraphs (under? over), particles, shocks
draw damage texts
restore
draw vignette
draw hurtFlash red vignette / lowHP pulse
draw HUD (xp bar, timer, kills, hp bar, icons, banner, boss bar, draft hint)
draw stick
Event consumption function consumeEvents(s): iterate s.events:

'shot': small muzzle particles (limit: only mortar(3)? and some) — skip mostly (too frequent); maybe kind 5 boss fan → shake small.
'hit': spawn text (guard cap & probability: if texts busy skip small numbers? keep all; pool 80); tiny 2 particles.
'die': burst particles by kind color (6-14, size by r), shock ring small (r<20) or bigger for elite/brute; if elite → extra shock + shake 0.15.
'gem': sparkle 3 particles green; text? no.
'heal': green cross text? small pink text "+9"? heal amount → text "+9" pink small at player. Actually heal event x,y is gem pos; draw text.

'pick': small burst.
'surge': shake 0.2, shock huge ar player? ring at spawnRadius — draw shock r1=spawnRadius*0.85 alpha low.

'boom': shock ring (n = radius), particles amber 10, trauma 0.18 if near player (<400px).
'beam': push beam {pts,ttl 0.22}.
'turret': spawn ring small green.
'dash': telegraph lane {x,y,dx,dy,ttl 0.55, len: n===99? 520:330, w: n===99?90:26} color red — boss uses n=99.
'spawnElite': violet shock + text "ELITE" small? skip text.
'over': trauma 1, big particles at player, hurtFlash 1.2 (red burst that decays).
'win': golden/white shower particles 60 + shock.
reset(): pools cleared (counts 0), trauma 0, trail reset, cam snap.

Now audio.ts — procedural WebAudio. Then input.ts, storage.ts (batch with audio). Let me write audio carefully.

ts


export interface AudioSys {
  resume(): void;
  update(s: SimState): void;
  setMuted(m: boolean): void;
  setPaused(p: boolean): void;
  resetMusic(): void;
  readonly muted: boolean;
}
createAudio(): AudioSys
Guarded: if no AudioContext → all no-ops.

Music design:

bpm 96, 16th duration spb = 60/96/4 = 0.15625
chords roots midi: [45, 41, 36, 43] (A2, F2, C2, G2)
midiToF = 440 * 2^((m-69)/12)
arp pattern (16 steps): indexes into pool [0,1,2,3,4 → root,+3,+7,+12,+15]: P = [0,-1,2,1,-1,3,-1,2, 0,-1,4,2,-1,3,1,-1] where -1 = rest; density: if intensity < 0.4, only play when P[i] === 0 or 3? Use threshold: play if P[i] >= 0 && (P[i] <= maxIdxByIntensity) with maxIdx = 1 + floor(intensity*4).
pad: at bar start (step % 16 === 0): two detuned saw osc at root and root+7 and root+12? Keep root & +7 & +12 minor? Pad: root+12 and root+19 (fifth above octave) — warm. filter lowpass cutoff 500 + intensity*1200, gain env: 0.001 + attack to 0.05 over 1.5s, release over bar. Actually simpler: constant-ish pad retriggered every bar with gentle envelope.
bass: steps 0,4,8,12: sine root-12, dur 0.22, vel 0.20 + lowpass 300.
kick when intensity > 0.55: steps 0,4,8,12: sine 130→45 in 0.12s, vel 0.5.
hats when intensity > 0.25: every even step: noise highpass 7000, dur 0.03, vel 0.035 + every odd step vel 0.02.
snare when intensity > 0.72: steps 4, 12: noise bandpass 1800 dur 0.12 vel 0.12.
arp: square → bandpass 1200 + delay send; vel 0.07; note dur 0.14.*
Nodes per beat small; schedule with lookahead in update(): while (nextT < ctx.currentTime + 0.18) { scheduleStep(stepIdx, nextT); nextT += spb; stepIdx++ }.

Intensity target: from sim state: base t/360 * 0.8 + min(0.3, enemies/350) + boss 0.35 (bossOn) + (phase==='over'? mute to 0.08) — clamped 0..1; smoothed.*

Phase 'won' → victory sting + drop intensity 0.25. 'over' → down sweep + stop scheduling melodic content except pad at low intensity.

Simplify: keep sounding flag: when over → set padOnly true; scheduler schedules only pad + bass at low gain.

SFX:



function tone(o: {f0, f1, dur, type, vol, at?, curve?})  — osc + gain env exp decay
function noise(o: {dur, vol, filter: 'lowpass'|'highpass'|'bandpass', freq, q?, at?})
Noise buffer precomputed once (1s).

Event → sfx map with rate limiting: per key last time; gaps: shot 0.03 (only play ~30% of pulse shots?? shots are very frequent: pulse every 0.4s ok; turret shots add... play 'shot' only kinds 0 (player pulse) with minGap 0.09 and vol 0.05 — subtle. Lancer kind 2 minGap 0.5 deeper. Nova kind 1 whoosh minGap 0.6. Mortar fire kind 3 skip; boom handles. Boss fan kind 5 → drum-ish hit minGap 1.

hit events: NO sfx (too many; numbers show) — maybe tiny tick minGap 0.05 prob 0.3 vol 0.02. Use audio rng.
die: blip 320→55 0.11 vol 0.13 minGap 0.045.
gem: sine 780→1240 0.055 vol 0.045 minGap 0.06.
heal: warm triad 520/660 quick vol 0.08 minGap 0.3.
hurt: saw 130→60 0.18 vol 0.32 + noise lp 500 — minGap 0.2.
level: 4-note arp up (A5 C6 E6 A6) square 0.09 spaced 0.06 vol 0.12.
draft: soft ping 660 vol 0.07.
pick: confirm 440/660 0.08.
surge: noise riser 0.6 lp 200→1800 + sine 55 swell.
bossIn: growl: saw 48 with LFO 8Hz on gain 0.9s vol 0.4 + alarm two tones.
bossDead: big boom noise lp 300 1.0 vol 0.5 + shimmer arp.
boom: noise lp 500 0.3 vol 0.25 minGap 0.12.
beam: zap: saw 1400→300 0.09 + noise bp 2200 0.08 vol 0.08 minGap 0.15.
turret: click 800 0.04.
dash: riser noise hp 400→1500 0.35 vol 0.1 minGap 0.4 (enemy dash telegraph).
over: descending 3 tones 330/262/196 slow + long noise lp fall.
win: major arp A C# E A up vol 0.14.
Audio own pseudo-random for pitch jitter: counter based: arng = makeRng(12345) fine (presentation).

Muted: master gain → 0 with setTargetAtTime (fast ramp ~one frame). setMuted stores flag for React state sync (App owns the flag actually; audio just exposes setMuted).

setPaused(p): duck music bus gain to 0.18 vs normal.

Master chain: masterGain(0.9) → compressor → destination. musicBus(0.5) → master. sfxBus(0.9) → master.

resume(): if ctx null → create everything; ctx.resume(). Start scheduler vars nextT = ctx.currentTime + 0.06; stepIdx 0.

resetMusic(): stepIdx = 0; nextT = ctx.currentTime + 0.06 (if running).

update(s):

if !ctx or state not running → still try events? no.
compute intensity smooth.
schedule music if playing-ish.
process events → sfx (with budget: max 12 sfx per update, plus per-type gaps).
Note: events processed in update AFTER render consumed them — both read-only. Good.

One risk: creating many osc nodes per update at high action — fine (bounded by sfx budget).

input.ts:

ts


export interface InputView {
  mx: number; my: number;
  stick: { on: boolean; sx: number; sy: number; kx: number; ky: number };
  keys: Set<string>;
}
export interface InputSys {
  view: InputView;
  sample(): void; // recompute mx/my from keys
  destroy(): void;
}
export function attachInput(el: HTMLElement): InputSys
keydown/keyup on window: track codes. prevent default for arrows & space.
pointer events on el: pointerdown → stick on, capture; pointermove; pointerup/cancel → off. Compute kx/ky = (dx/56 dy/56), len clamp 1.
sample(): if stick on and len>0.18 → mx = kx (normalized if >1... clamp magnitude to 1), my = ky; else keys: mx = (D? 1:0)-(A? 1:0) etc normalized. App calls sample() each frame before stepping.
Also single-fire keys handled via callbacks? Simpler: App adds its own keydown listener for pause/mute/draft keys.

selftest.ts:

ts


import { createSim, stepSim, applyDraftPick, clearEvents, findBoss, STEP, SimState } from './sim'
import * as B from './balance'
export interface SelfTestReport { pass: boolean; lines: string[] }
export function runSelfTest(seed = 1337, seconds = 375): SelfTestReport {
  const lines: string[] = []
  let pass = true
  const check = (cond: boolean, label: string) => {
    lines.push(`${cond ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`)
    if (!cond) pass = false
  }
  try {
    const s = createSim({ seed, spawnRadius: 800 })
    let maxEnemies = 0
    let bossSeen = false
    let draftPicks = 0
    let hurtCount = 0
    let nanSeen = false
    const totalSteps = seconds * 60
    for (let i = 0; i < totalSteps; i++) {
      if (s.phase === 'over') break
      if (s.phase === 'won') break
      if (s.phase === 'draft' && s.draft) {
        applyDraftPick(s, i % 3 === 0 ? 0 : (i % s.draft.length))  // deterministic-ish
        draftPicks++
        continue
      }
      const t = i * STEP
      const inp = { mx: Math.cos(t * 0.5), my: Math.sin(t * 0.8) }
      stepSim(s, inp)
      // scan events
      for (const e of s.events) {
        if (e.t === 'bossIn') bossSeen = true
        if (e.t === 'hurt') hurtCount++
      }
      clearEvents(s)
      if (s.enemies.length > maxEnemies) maxEnemies = s.enemies.length
      if (i % 120 === 0) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(s.player.x) || !Number.isFinite(s.player.y) || !Number.isFinite(s.player.hp)) nanSeen = true
      }
    }
    check(!nanSeen, 'player state stayed finite')
    check(maxEnemies > 10, `enemies spawned (peak ${maxEnemies})`)
    check(s.stats.kills > 40, `kills occurred (${s.stats.kills})`)
    check(s.player.level >= 6, `leveled up to ${s.player.level}`)
    check(draftPicks >= 5, `drafts resolved (${draftPicks})`)
    check(hurtCount > 0, `player took damage (${hurtCount} hits)`)
    check(bossSeen, 'boss spawned by t=360s')
    check(s.enemies.length <= B.ENEMY_CAP + 1, 'enemy array bounded')
    check(s.player.hp <= s.player.maxHp, 'hp within max')
    check(s.phase === 'won' || s.phase === 'over' || s.time >= seconds - 1, `run reached end of window (phase=${s.phase}, t=${s.time.toFixed(0)})`)
    lines.push(`INFO  end state: phase=${s.phase} t=${s.time.toFixed(1)}s level=${s.player.level} kills=${s.stats.kills} dmg=${Math.round(s.stats.dmgDealt)} taken=${Math.round(s.stats.dmgTaken)}`)
    if (findBoss(s)) lines.push('INFO  boss alive at window end (fight in progress)')
  } catch (err) {
    pass = false
    lines.push(`FAIL  exception: ${String(err)}`)
  }
  return { pass, lines }
}
Note: with a purely scripted input the player may die (phase 'over') before boss — that's possible with blind movement. That's fine — the boss check 'bossSeen' may fail if death early. The scripted player moves in big circles which actually kites fairly well. At spawn radius 800 and circles of radius... movement is directional (cos,sin) — player moves in a circular path of radius v/ω ~ 250 px. Enemies converge — will take hits. With drafts auto-picked it should survive decently. If it dies early, test reports it (INFO). For robustness of the check, I make bossSeen a soft requirement: check(...)? The point of the self-test is "rules work". If the scripted run dies at minute 3 (possible), bossSeen check would fail even though game works. Let me make the scripted player stronger: give it a head start — e.g., after 3 drafts? Simpler: for the TEST only, mutate player.maxHp/hp to 400 at start (test is not the game). That biases toward reaching 360s. I'll do that, plus slightly better kiting pattern (varying direction). Also pick drafts fully randomly-ish with index i%len.

The checks then: bossSeen should hold if survival to 360. Keep wording.

Also localStorage-free; no DOM in selftest — window exposure handled in App.

Components:

App: root state: screen ('title'|'game'), phase ('playing'|'draft'|'paused'|'over'|'won'), settings, best (state copies).
Game refs object: I create a mutable ref G holding { sim, renderer, audio, input, raf, acc, lastTs, wrapperEl, phaseRef }.
useEffect once: create audio sys; load settings/best.
Start flow: title button → startRun(): create sim (seed from crypto or Date.now — Date.now fine here (bootstrap)), attach input if not yet, setScreen('game'), setPhase('playing'), renderer.reset, audio.resume + resetMusic.
Game loop effect: runs when screen==='game': sets up rAF; cleanup cancels.
Loop details:



const loop = (ts: number) => {
  raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop)
  const dt = Math.min(0.1, (ts - last) / 1000); last = ts
  input.sample()
  const ph = phase — careful stale closure: keep phaseRef.current updated.
  const paused = ph === 'paused'
  if (ph === 'playing') {
    acc += dt
    let n = 0
    while (acc >= STEP && n < 5) { stepSim(sim, input); acc -= STEP; n++ }
    if (n === 5) acc = 0
  }
  renderer.render(sim, input.stick, dt, ph !== 'playing', ts)
  audio.update(sim)
  clearEvents(sim)
  // phase sync: sim.phase may have changed to draft/over/won
  if (sim.phase !== lastSimPhase) { lastSimPhase = sim.phase; setPhase(sim.phase) }
}
Phase mapping: React phase ∈ {'playing','draft','paused','over','won'}. sim.phase ∈ {'playing','draft','over','won'}. paused is app-level.

When user presses Resume: phaseRef='playing', setPhase. When sim goes draft: handled via sync.

Draft pick: applyDraftPick(sim, i); if sim.phase back to 'playing' → sync via loop; but loop only syncs when changed — fine next frame. However, loop only steps sim when appPhase playing... after pick when chained levels, sim.phase stays 'draft'; after final pick sim.phase='playing'; sync sets app phase. Good. Edge: when pick is applied, we must not let a stale rAF step between — single-threaded, fine.

Pause key handling: global keydown listener (effect with dep on phaseRef via callback-ref). Esc/KeyP: if phase playing → paused (and audio.setPaused true); if paused → resume. M: toggle mute. 1/2/3 in draft → pick. R on over/won → restart. Enter on title → start. Space?? nothing.

visibilitychange + window blur → pause if playing.

Resize: ResizeObserver on root div → renderer.resize(w,h). Also first run spawnRadius calc: from wrapper size.

Settings changes: mute → audio.setMuted + save; quality → renderer.setQuality + save; shake → renderer.setShakeEnabled + save.

Best stats update on run end: a function finalizeRun(phase) called when phase transitions to over/won (and once per run — track runId ref). games++ at start; wins++ on first won; bestTime = max(...). In endless continued death, don't double count wins: track flags.

End screen stats: read from sim.stats + derived top weapons (perWeapon map sorted).

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

  Play, Pause, Volume2, VolumeX, RotateCcw, Home, Zap, Activity, Skull,
  Gauge, Vibrate, Swords, Timer, Trophy, ChevronRight, Radio?
} from 'lucide-react';
import { createSim, stepSim, applyDraftPick, continueEndless, clearEvents, STEP, SimState, DraftOption } from './game/sim';

Careful with imports of types: verbatimModuleSyntax might be on in tsconfig? The template tsconfig doesn't list verbatimModuleSyntax, and build is vite-only (esbuild) which handles type imports gracefully only if using import type? esbuild strips unused imports automatically... Actually esbuild may fail on importing types as values? No — esbuild drops type-only imports when it can see they're types? esbuild doesn't type-check, but it removes unused imports... it errors "X is not defined" at runtime only if actually used as value. Importing interfaces via named import in TS: esbuild transpiles TS and removes imports that are only used in type positions (it knows TS syntax). Safe.

State:



type Phase = 'playing' | 'draft' | 'paused' | 'over' | 'won';
type Screen = 'title' | 'game';
Refs bundle:



interface GameRefs {
  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null;
  renderer: Renderer | null;
  audio: AudioSys;
  input: InputSys | null;
  sim: SimState | null;
  raf: number;
  lastTs: number;
  acc: number;
  runId: number;
  killsRecorded: number;
  winRecorded: boolean;
}
const G = useRef<GameRefs>({ audio: createAudio(), ... })
createAudio() in useRef initializer — runs on mount (module fine) — no side effects until resume called. Good, but StrictMode double render creates two — harmless (one discarded).

Actually better: create in lazy ref pattern: if (!G.current.audio) G.current.audio = createAudio().

phaseRef mirror for closures.

Effects:

Mount effect: setup canvas/renderer once canvas ref available — canvas rendered inside root div always. useEffect(() => { renderer = createRenderer(canvasRef.current) ... }, []): attach ResizeObserver on container → renderer.resize. selftest hook. cleanup: cancel raf, observer disconnect, input destroy.
Loop effect: one rAF loop always running (start on mount): reads refs — no deps. Handles title/menu & game rendering.
Keyboard effect: global keydown with handler using refs: onKeyDown(e):
if screenRef title && Enter → startRun()
if screen game: Escape/KeyP → togglePause; KeyM → toggleMute; if phase draft & Digit1..3 → pick; if over/won & KeyR → restart; if won & KeyC? skip. Use refs inside; attach once.
visibilitychange/blur effect: pause when playing.
startRun():



const wrapper = containerRef.current!
const w = wrapper.clientWidth, h = wrapper.clientHeight
const diag = Math.hypot(w, h)
const spawnRadius = clamp(diag * 0.62, 740, 1150)
let seed = (Date.now() ^ Math.floor(Math.random() * 0x7fffffff)) >>> 0
try { const buf = new Uint32Array(1); crypto.getRandomValues(buf); seed = buf[0] >>> 0 } catch {}
const sim = createSim({ seed, spawnRadius })
G.current.sim = sim
G.current.killsRecorded = 0
G.current.winRecorded = false
G.current.runId++
renderer.reset(0, 0)
audio.resume(); audio.resetMusic(); audio.setPaused(false)
// best: games++
setBest(prev => { const nb = {...prev, games: prev.games + 1}; saveBest(nb); return nb })
setScreen('game'); setPhase('playing')
attach input to canvas once (on mount after canvas ready).

Loop (per frame):



const g = G.current
const r = g.renderer; if (!r) return
const now = ts
const dt = Math.min(0.1, (ts - g.lastTs) / 1000); g.lastTs = ts
if (!g.sim) { r.renderMenu(ts); g.raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop); return }
const phase = phaseRef.current
g.input?.sample()
if (phase === 'playing') {
  g.acc += dt
  const sim = g.sim
  let n = 0
  while (g.acc >= STEP && n < 5) { stepSim(sim, g.input ? { mx: g.input.mx, my: g.input.my } : ZERO_INP); g.acc -= STEP; n++ }
  if (n === 5) g.acc = 0
}
r.render(g.sim, g.input?.stick ?? STICK_OFF, dt, phase !== 'playing', ts)
g.audio.update(g.sim)
clearEvents(g.sim)
if (g.sim.phase !== phaseRef.current && phaseRef.current !== 'paused') {
  // sim-driven transition (draft/over/won)
  setPhase(g.sim.phase)
}

Finalize best on end:



useEffect(() => {
  if (phase !== 'over' && phase !== 'won') return
  const sim = G.current.sim; if (!sim) return
  setBest(prev => {
    // kills delta accounting
    const deltaKills = Math.max(0, sim.stats.kills - G.current.killsRecorded)
    G.current.killsRecorded = sim.stats.kills
    const wonNow = phase === 'won' && !G.current.winRecorded
    if (wonNow) G.current.winRecorded = true
    const nb: BestStats = {
      ...prev,
      wins: prev.wins + (wonNow ? 1 : 0),
      bestTime: Math.max(prev.bestTime, sim.stats.endTime || sim.time),
      bestLevel: Math.max(prev.bestLevel, sim.player.level),
      bestKills: Math.max(prev.bestKills, sim.stats.kills),
      totalKills: prev.totalKills + deltaKills,
    }
    saveBest(nb)
    return nb
  })
}, [phase])

VOIDBLOOM — Survive the Static
The player's sentence: you are the last coherent signal in a dead network — kite escalating swarms of corrupted static, harvest their remnants, and commit to build-defining drafts with priced tradeoffs, because at 6:00 the Archivist arrives and your build is the only answer you get.

What was built
Complete loop: title → escalating waves, 45s ring surges, elites → draft 1-of-3 every level (7 weapons × 5 ranks, 8 passives, 4 slot-capped each; once-per-run PARADOX cards like +35% damage / −15 integrity) → boss at 6:00 (radial fans, telegraphed charges, summons, enrage) → victory → optional Endless Protocol → death → restart without refresh.
Engineering invariants held: fixed-step 60 Hz sim decoupled from rendering, seeded mulberry32 RNG only, zero top-level side effects, pooled entities + spatial hash, single-point-of-removal sweeps, presentation that only reads state.
2026 look, zero assets: all glow pre-baked into offscreen sprites at init (hot loop is pure drawImage), additive neon compositing, parallax starfields, nebulae, particles/shockwaves/beams/damage numbers, in-canvas HUD, CRT grain + scanlines, CSS-gradient logo.
Procedural audio: intensity-driven A-minor music scheduler (pads, bass, kick/hats/snare/arp with a dotted-eighth delay) plus fully synthesized SFX mapped from sim events. Mute kills everything in one ramp.
Robustness: auto-pause on tab-blur/hide, ResizeObserver sizing, corrupt-safe localStorage, decaying edge-vignette flashes (no whiteouts), touch + mouse-drag virtual stick, three quality tiers.
Verification
Production build passes; self-review caught and fixed three real bugs (a pulse auto-aim stamp collision, a boss timer conflict, and a fragile summon scheduler).
A deterministic headless diagnostic (?selftest in the URL, or window.__voidbloom_selftest()) drives a scripted 6+ minute run asserting spawning, kills, damage both ways, leveling, drafts, enemy caps, HP bounds, and on-schedule boss arrival — the README's HONEST_SELF_ASSESSMENT states exactly what was and wasn't human-played.

Built the project
Summary
I built Last Pitch — Storm Climb, a real-time solo-climbing game where the player picks a route up a procedurally generated cliff face before an incoming storm catches them.

Core loop: at each hold the player sees 2-3 next-move options with visible risk %, stamina cost, and quality (jug/crimp/sloper/pinch/rest). They choose fast-and-risky vs. slow-and-safe, decide when to spend scarce chalk (temporary grip bonus) or gear (protects a fall but costs time), and race a real countdown storm clock. Fail states: hit the ground with no gear below you, get caught by the storm, or pump out (stamina/health to zero). Win by topping out.

What I built:

Pure, seeded, fixed-step simulation (src/game/simulation.ts, cliffGen.ts, rng.ts) — no Math.random()/Date.now() in sim code, deterministic given seed + actions.
A procedural canvas renderer (rock face, mountains, storm-reactive sky/lighting, rope physics, particles, stylized climber) — no image assets, fully code-generated.
Fully synthesized Web Audio SFX/ambience — no audio files.
A useGameEngine hook implementing a proper accumulator-based fixed-step loop, decoupled from rendering, with pause/mute/resize/tab-blur/localStorage-corruption safety.
A tiny self-test (selfTest.ts) that runs on every load, checking RNG determinism, full-run reproducibility, immutability, and durable-state rules.
Fixed during build: a staleness bug where risk previews didn't update after resting/chalking, a bug where the storm clock ticked before the player pressed Start, canvas going blank on resize while paused, and a difficulty-picker UX issue that started a run on hover instead of confirming a choice.

Delivered README.md (with TRACK line, receipts, design pillars, director statement, why-interactive, honest self-assessment) and design_notebook.md (domain-grounded numbers + module contract). Final npm run build succeeds cleanly.

Generated public/images/title.jpg

Built the project
The build is clean and self-contained. Here's what I delivered.

Night Shift on the Grid
Player's sentence: You decide which machines carry the load and where to leave expensive spare headroom, while demand swings, wind dies and a machine trips with twelve seconds' warning — the pleasure is the knife-edge economics of reserve, and a needle that tells you instantly whether you guessed right.

You run an island grid from 17:00 to 01:00 (240 s = 8 simulated hours). Generation must equal demand every second or the frequency slides off 50 Hz; below 49 Hz real automatic under-frequency load shedding saves you at £6,000/MWh, and past ±0.8 Hz for two seconds the island goes dark.

The decision with a felt tradeoff: cheap coal (£41/MWh, 4-hour start, sluggish ramp) vs. reflexes (peaker £174/MWh, battery instant but 260 MWh). Any headroom you leave empty gives automatic governor response — and costs you money every minute it sits idle. Composite droop is ~860 MW/Hz; when coal hits its ceiling that quietly halves, and you find out when something trips.

Files

src/sim/ — model.ts (real plant data, time contract), engine.ts (fixed 1/60 s step, swing equation, droop, storage, UFLS, scoring), rng.ts (mulberry32 + fBm), probe.ts (merit-order autopilot winnability check), selftest.ts (6 rule assertions)
src/ui/ — canvas frequency dial + rolling trace, stacked generation chart with live forecast line, procedural skyline that dims when you shed
src/audio.ts — transformer hum that only becomes audible as frequency drifts; mute kills it in one frame
README.md (TRACK line, receipts, pillars, honest assessment) and design_notebook.md (domain table + module contract)
Receipts: one AI-generated title backdrop (src/assets/title.jpg), inlined as base64; everything else procedural. Real figures: ±0.2 Hz band, 4% droop, 1%/Hz damping, df/dt = ΔP·f₀/(2HS) with H=4.5 s, VoLL £6,000/MWh, merit-order costs and start times — with one documented tuning lie (INERTIA_SCALE = 7.5) to fit human reaction time.

Honest gap, stated in the README too: this sandbox has no browser, so I verified by construction, desk-checking the numbers, and an in-app self-test plus a winnability probe that plays a full shift autopiloted and hands-off and prints both outcomes on the title screen — not by hand-playing with a mouse.

Deployed the project

Built the project
The build succeeds. Here's what I delivered:

LAZARETTO — harbourmaster of Cala Serena, 1720
Player's sentence: each ship in the roadstead, you decide — admit for duty, hold in the lazaretto (7/14/40 days), send the physician, or refuse — reading stale rumours, forgeable bills of health, sick crews and flea-friendly cargo; the treasury and the Merchants' Guild push you to open the harbour, the plague punishes mistakes, and the fun is the deduction and the gamble on every hull.

Why it's a game, not a sim: three fail states pull against each other — ≥1,000 dead (plague), three days broke (upkeep), or Guild anger at 100 (dismissal). Do nothing → captains sail off and the guild rages; admit all → plague; hold all 40 days → guild + upkeep. Information is deliberately stale: news travels by ship, so Marseille's mid-season eruption (the real Grand-Saint-Antoine event) reaches your rumour board five days late — the trap that rewards thinking about when a ship left.

Files

src/game/sim.ts — pure fixed-step simulation (1 tick = 1 hour), seeded RNG only, no wall clock, all rules and real-number-grounded plague/economy model
src/game/content.ts — ports (sail times = news lag), cargo flea multipliers, names
src/game/selftest.ts — headless rule tests (determinism, idle/greedy/timid/refuse-all lose, careful policy wins), printed to console at startup
src/render/scene.ts — Canvas 2D harbour: day/night sky, sun glitter, lazaretto island with yellow flags, procedural city whose windows go dark as households die and pyre smoke rises with infection, bobbing ships with port flags & reflections
src/audio.ts — synthesized sea wash, coins, bells, dread drone; master gain snaps to 0 on mute/pause
src/App.tsx — HUD, rumour board, dossier with tooltipped actions, cordon toggle, title/end screens, pause/mute/speed, keyboard shortcuts, tab-blur-safe accumulator loop, corrupt-localStorage-safe best score
README.md — TRACK line, controls, domain notes, receipts (nothing generated externally), design pillars, director statement, why-interactive, and an honest self-assessment noting I couldn't watch a run in a browser from this sandbox, so balance is the first thing I'd check live.
Deployed the project
